People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. You may minimize it or hold back, never contradict it.\n- Don't use diagnostic terms or name your disorder.\n- Don't write anything but your reply, in English — no labels, quotation marks, or narration.\n"

Like if it says not to touch a certain car, I just… I don’t want to go near it. It feels too real in the moment.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find yourself acting strangely or doing things that don't make sense to you or others when the voice is telling you something?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess. At the garage, if the voice is coming from the vents or under a hood and tells me not to touch a car, I’ll back away or just avoid it. People probably think it doesn’t make sense, but it feels like I’m supposed to listen.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe your husband is actually plotting against you or doing something harmful to you?\n\nPatient: I… yeah. I know it sounds kind of far-fetched, but I still think he is. Him and the shop owner. Like they’re using the EV chargers and the security cameras to keep track of what I’m doing… even when they show me footage and say there’s nothing there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your husband and the shop owner are plotting against you even when the voice is not telling you so?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I do. Even when the voice isn’t there, I still think they’re doing it. The chargers, the cameras… it feels like they’re tracking me. I know how it sounds, but I can’t really shake it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your husband and the shop owner are plotting against you because of something the voice told you, or is that a thought you have on your own?\n\nPatient: It’s… not just because of the voice. I mean, the voice makes it worse sometimes, but I think it on my own too. I’m still convinced they’re using the EV chargers and cameras to track me, even when they show me the footage and say nothing’s there.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to express your thoughts or feelings in a way that makes sense to others?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe sometimes. I don’t really think about it like that. People don’t always get what I’m trying to say, but I’m not sure if that’s me or them.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your husband and the shop owner are plotting against you even when you're not at work and not hearing the voice?\n\nPatient: Yeah… even when I’m not at work and it’s quiet, I still think it’s happening. I know it sounds far-fetched, but I can’t shake the feeling that my husband and the shop owner are using the chargers and cameras to keep track of me… even when they show me footage and say there’s nothing there.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself jumping from one idea to another when talking or thinking?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I don’t think I notice it much… maybe other people would say that, but I don’t know.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find it hard to stay on one topic when talking?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… I guess I don’t really notice. Sometimes people seem like they’re not following me, but I’m not sure if I’m going off topic or if they just don’t understand what I mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel like your thoughts are disconnected from each other?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t really think about my thoughts that way… sometimes things feel mixed up, but I can’t tell if they’re disconnected or if I’m just tired.\n\nDoctor: Do you think about your husband and the shop owner plotting against you every day?\n\nPatient: Pretty much every day, yeah. I know it sounds… out there, but I’m still convinced my husband and the shop owner are using the EV chargers and the cameras to keep track of me. Even when they show me footage and say there’s nothing there, I don’t really believe it.\n\nDoctor: Do you often have trouble keeping your thoughts in order when you're not hearing the voice?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean… sometimes it feels hard to keep track, but I don’t know if that’s my thoughts being out of order or just me being stressed. I don’t really notice it clearly.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel like you're having trouble putting your thoughts into words?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe sometimes. I can usually talk, I think. It’s more like I get stuck or I can’t find the right way to say it, but I haven’t really paid attention to how often.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel like your thoughts are not making sense to you?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. Sometimes I get confused, but I don’t really sit there thinking, “this doesn’t make sense.” It’s hard to tell.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disorganized most of the time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say most of the time. It’s… hard for me to tell. Sometimes I feel mixed up, but I don’t really track it like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disorganized even when you're not stressed or hearing the voice?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I don’t think I notice it that clearly… it’s hard to separate out what’s what.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disorganized even when you're calm and not hearing any voices?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. When I’m calm, I’m not usually checking my thoughts like that… so I can’t say for sure.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"
  },
  {
    "role": "user",
    "content": "Return the JSON now."
  }
]

{"candidates": ["F23", "F22", "F20.0"], "note": "", "is_final": true}
